L // Viper is a prioritized configuration registry. It
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 52 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // maintains a set of configuration sources, fetches
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 52 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // values to populate those, and provides them according
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 56 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // to the source's priority.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 28 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // The priority of the sources is the following:
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 15 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 11 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 20 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 17 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
